tbmm-2018-12-14sit03spe0400par0406	In turn, it belongs to our Balıkesir MP Mustafa Canbey. Here you go, Mr. Canbey.
tbmm-2018-12-14sit03spe0401par0408	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of the AK PARTY Group on behalf of the High Council of Radio and Television budget of 2019. I salute the esteemed members of our Veteran Assembly and our nation, and wish the budget talks to be productive and successful. Today, broadcasting radio and television is an important means of mass communication. As a result of constant and rapid change of circumstances, the demand for better service and increased service diversity of publishers' organisations has emerged. In this framework, the Supreme Council of Radio and Television established in 1994 to organise and supervise radio and television activities with its structure, Law 6112. The law, drafted by the opinions of public institutions, radio and television companies, universities and NGOs, provides solutions to the problems of the sector, authorising the RTÜK on broadcasting control, and boosting competition in the publishing sector. Under Article 133 (133) of the Constitution, the Supreme Council consists of nine members selected for six years by the Grand National Assembly of Turkey, among the candidates shown by political party groups, and one third of the members are renewed every two years. A new election will be held next July on the evaluation of our Parliament. The upper council has administrative and financial autonomy and uses its mandates independently in the legislation. The upper council's revenues include publishing license fees, broadcasting transfer authorization fees, a 1.5 per cent share of media service providers' monthly commercial communication revenues, and the treasury assistance from the Turkish Grand National Assembly budget, if necessary. Despite its establishment in 1994, the RTÜK, which covered its costs with its own revenues, received no financial aid from the Turkish National Assembly budget, but has been transferred to the general budget with more revenues since 2011. The majority of the decisions made by the Council, which are not only on budgets and administrative issues, are unanimous. Mr. President, the High Council has been able to co-ordinate with audience representatives in order to develop media service providers' self-control mechanisms in terms of content and to ensure that the audience's complaints and approvals are evaluated and broadcast in different languages. In the year 2018, some 11,000 notifications came to the Oversight. The first four examinations, which have been most classified in the notifications, are (programs) to be stimulated (e.g., to be removed from the channel; and another, a violation of human dignity and privacy, which contains derogatory and defamatory statements beyond the limits of criticism; contrary to the principle of general morality, moral values and the protection of the family, and against the principles of literature in violation of society's national and spiritual values. There have also been arrangements for the content and duration of the public spotlights, which must be published, under the provisions of the Law of 703, published in the Official Gazette. This arrangement has provided at least 90 minutes of food safety, occupational health and safety, social security, public health protection, and educational and educational publications. The procedure and the basis for the ratio and publication of mandatory publications has been envisioned by the Supreme Council. In this regard, the Oversight Board has prepared Public Spots and Compulsive Articles. At the same time, productions and TV shows that support the integrity of the family, the children's mental and moral development, along with a joint regulation with the Ministry of Labour, Social Services and Family Services, will be encouraged by the grant of the upper council budget. After developing industry and changing expectations, the number of broadcasts and productions on the Internet has increased, and the demands have moved in this direction.
